
========== day1_1_morning ==========

[start]
  KO ({name}): *4월. 벚꽃이 흩날린다.*
  EN (Me): *April. Cherry blossoms scatter in the breeze.*

[start_2]
  KO ({name}): *교복이 아직 뻣뻣하다. 풀 냄새가 은은하게 난다. 어제 다림질했는데도 접은 자국이 지 멋대로 남아 있다. 교복이랑 벌써 사이가 안 좋다.*
  EN (Me): *My uniform is still stiff. There's a faint smell of starch. Even though I ironed it yesterday, the creases have a mind of their own. Already off to a bad start with this uniform.*

[start_3]
  KO ({name}): *오늘부터 이 학교 학생이다. 전학. 처음이다.*
  EN (Me): *Starting today, I'm a student at this school. A transfer. First time.*

[start_3b]
  KO ({name}): *손이 주머니 안에서 핸드폰 모서리를 만지작거린다. 인정하기 싫지만, 긴장할 때 나오는 습관이다.*
  EN ({name}): *My hand fidgets with the edge of my phone inside my pocket. I hate to admit it, but it's a habit that only comes out when I'm nervous.*

[start_4]
  KO ({name}): *주머니에서 핸드폰이 울린다. 이전 학교 단체 카톡방. 화면에 뜬 이름을 보고 — 주머니에 도로 넣었다. 아직은.*
  EN (Me): *My phone buzzes in my pocket. The old school group chat. I see a name flash on the screen — and shove the phone back in. Not yet.*

[start_5]
  KO ({name}): *벚꽃잎이 코끝을 스친다. — 재채기가 나올 뻔했다. 드라마면 여기서 예쁜 애가 부딪히면서 등장하는 건데.*
  EN (Me): *A cherry blossom petal brushes my nose. — I almost sneezed. If this were a drama, this is the part where some pretty girl bumps into me.*

[start_6]
  KO ({name}): *현실은 그냥 재채기다.*
  EN (Me): *Reality is just a sneeze.*

[name_input_scene]
  KO (시스템): 전학생, 당신의 이름은?
  EN (System): Transfer student, what is your name?

[gate_1]
  KO ({name}): *낯선 교문 앞에 섰다. 꽤 큰 학교다. 건물이 깨끗하고 운동장도 넓다.*
  EN (Me): *I'm standing in front of an unfamiliar school gate. It's a pretty big school. Clean buildings, spacious grounds.*

[gate_2]
  KO ({name}): *학생들이 삼삼오오 교문을 지나간다. 나만 교복이 새하얗게 뻣뻣하다. 광고판 같다 — '저 새로 왔어요'.*
  EN (Me): *Students pass through the gate in small groups. My uniform is the only one blindingly crisp. Like a billboard — 'I'm new here.'*

[gate_3]
  KO ({name}): *교문 앞에 한 여학생이 서 있다. 검은 긴머리를 가볍게 넘기며 — 안경 너머로 걸음이 여유롭다. 서두르는 기색이 전혀 없다.*
  EN (Me): *A girl is standing by the gate. She casually flips her long black hair — behind her glasses, her steps are relaxed. Not a hint of hurry.*

[seoyeon_meet_1]
  KO (서연): 전학생? {name} 맞지?
  EN (Seoyeon): Transfer student? You're {name}, right?

[seoyeon_meet_2]
  KO ({name}): *목소리가 차분하다. 차갑진 않은데, 모든 걸 예상하고 있다는 톤.*
  EN (Me): *Her voice is calm. Not cold, but measured — like she already knows how this conversation ends.*

[seoyeon_meet_3]
  KO ({name}): 맞는데. 어떻게 알았어?
  EN ({name}): That's me. How'd you know?

[seoyeon_meet_4]
  KO (서연): 학생회장이거든. 새 학생 안내는 내 일이야.
  EN (Seoyeon): I'm the student council president. Guiding new students is my job.

[seoyeon_meet_5]
  KO (서연): 따라와. 학교 안내해 줄게.
  EN (Seoyeon): Follow me. I'll show you around.

[seoyeon_meet_6]
  KO ({name}): 학생회장이 직접? 과분한데.
  EN ({name}): The president herself? I'm honored.

[seoyeon_meet_7]
  KO (서연): 과분한 건지 아닌 건지는 내가 정해.
  EN (Seoyeon): Whether you're honored or not — that's for me to decide.

[seoyeon_meet_8]
  KO ({name}): *피식 웃는다. 입꼬리만 올라가는 웃음. 계산된 건지 원래 그런 건지 — 아직 모르겠다.*
  EN (Me): *She smirks. Just the corner of her lips lifting. Whether it's calculated or just who she is — I can't tell yet.*

[seoyeon_meet_9]
  KO ({name}): *서연이 교문 쪽으로 걸으며 손짓한다.*
  EN (Me): *Seoyeon walks toward the gate, beckoning with a wave.*

[seoyeon_choice]
  KO ({name}): *어떻게 반응할까?*
  EN (Me): *What do I say to that?*
  KO choices: ["혹시 나한테 관심 있어서 먼저 나온 거 아니고?","가이드비는 얼마야?"]
  EN choices: ["Don't tell me you came out early because you're interested in me?","How much is the guide fee?"]

[choice_flirt_1]
  KO ({name}): *서연이 걸음을 멈추고 돌아본다. 놀라는 게 아니라 — 재밌다는 표정.*
  EN (Me): *Seoyeon stops walking and turns around. Not surprised — more amused.*

[choice_flirt_2]
  KO (서연): 와. 첫날부터 안 지네. 이름 기억해 둘게.
  EN (Seoyeon): Wow. First day and you're already not backing down. I'll remember your name.

[choice_flirt_3]
  KO ({name}): 점수제야?
  EN ({name}): A point system?

[choice_flirt_4]
  KO (서연): 이 학교에서 나한테 말대꾸하는 사람, 네가 처음이야.
  EN (Seoyeon): You're the first person at this school to talk back to me.

[choice_flirt_5]
  KO ({name}): 영광인데?
  EN ({name}): I'm flattered.

[choice_flirt_6]
  KO (서연): 아직 영광인지 재앙인지 모르는 거지.
  EN (Seoyeon): You don't know yet if it's flattery or disaster.

[choice_flirt_7]
  KO ({name}): *웃고 있다. 진짜 재밌어하는 것 같다. 첫날부터 이런 텐션의 학생회장이라니.*
  EN (Me): *She's smiling. She seems genuinely entertained. A student council president with this kind of energy on day one.*

[choice_joke_1]
  KO (서연): 공짜야. — 대신 나중에 이자 붙어.
  EN (Seoyeon): It's free. — But the interest builds up later.

[choice_joke_2]
  KO ({name}): 무서운 학생회장이네.
  EN ({name}): That's one scary student council president.

[choice_joke_3]
  KO (서연): 적어도 지루하지는 않을 거야.
  EN (Seoyeon): At least it won't be boring.

[hallway_1]
  KO ({name}): *서연이 복도를 걸으며 학교를 안내한다. 스치는 학생들이 서연에게 인사를 건넨다. 서연은 하나하나 눈인사로 받는다.*
  EN (Me): *Seoyeon guides me through the hallway. Passing students greet her. She acknowledges each one with a nod.*

[hallway_2]
  KO ({name}): *인기가 많은 건지 무서운 건지 — 아마 둘 다.*
  EN (Me): *Popular or feared — probably both.*

[hallway_3]

  EN (Seoyeon): Third-year building is here, library's over there. Snack bar is in the basement.

[hallway_4]
  KO ({name}): *서연이 주머니에서 열쇠를 꺼내 살짝 흔든다.*
  EN (Me): *Seoyeon pulls out a key from her pocket and gives it a little shake.*

[hallway_5]
  KO (서연): 학생회장이면 열 수 있는 문이 몇 개 있거든.

  EN (Me): *She hesitated — just half a second. Probably nothing, but it stays with me.*

  EN (Me): *We arrive in front of the classroom.*

  EN (Seoyeon): Here it is. Go on in.

  EN ({name}): Will you guide me at lunch too?

  EN (Me): *Seoyeon turns and gives a light wave. She doesn't look back.*

[hallway_14]

  EN (Me): *Her posture is straight. Perfectly straight. — Like she's practiced it.*

  EN (Me): *I open the classroom door. Eyes pour onto me.*

  EN (Me): *I expected it, but the weight of 30 stares is still heavy.*

[classroom_dain_2]

  EN (Me): *Brown bob cut, volleyball jersey still on. A voice brimming with energy. She's half-standing from her seat. The kid next to her is pulling her down, but it's no use.*

[classroom_dain_3]
  KO (담임): 다인아, 좀 조용히... 그리고 교실에서 유니폼 좀 그만 입고.
  EN (Homeroom Teacher): Dain, please quiet down... And stop wearing your jersey in class.

[classroom_dain_4]
  KO (다인): 아 쌤, 체육복 갈아입기 귀찮은데 어떡해요. 아무튼 전학생 반갑다!
  EN (Dain): Aw, teach! Changing out of my jersey is such a hassle, what can I do? Anyway, welcome, new kid!

[classroom_dain_5]
  KO ({name}): *볼륨 조절이 안 되는 타입이다. 근데 표정이 진짜 반가워하는 거라 미워할 수가 없다.*
  EN (Me): *She has no volume control. But her face is so genuinely happy that you can't dislike her.*

[classroom_yuna_1]
  KO ({name}): *시선을 돌리니, 창가 맨 뒤쪽에 한 여학생이 턱을 괸 채 창밖을 보고 있다. 자기소개에 관심이 없는 것 같다.*
  EN (Me): *I shift my gaze and spot a girl in the far back by the window, resting her chin on her hand, staring outside. She seems uninterested in my introduction.*

[classroom_yuna_2]
  KO ({name}): *은백색 머리. 붉은 눈이 나른하게 반쯤 감겨 있다. 팔에 타투가 빼곡하다. — 이쪽을 쳐다보지도 않는다. 전학생이 뭐 대수냐는 듯이.*
  EN (Me): *Silver-white hair. Red eyes, half-closed and drowsy. Tattoos cover her arms. — She doesn't even glance this way. As if a transfer student is nothing special.*

[classroom_yuna_3]
  KO ({name}): *주변 학생들이 유나 쪽으로 시선을 주지 않는다. 의도적으로. 마치 그 자리에 아무도 없는 것처럼.*
  EN (Me): *The students around her don't look in Yuna's direction. Deliberately. As if no one's sitting there at all.*

[classroom_yuna_4]
  KO ({name}): *앞줄에서 누군가 속삭인다. '...쟤한테 말 걸지 마, 무섭거든.' — 유나의 귀에 안 들렸을 리 없는 거리다. 근데 표정이 안 변한다. 익숙한 거다.*
  EN (Me): *Someone in the front row whispers. '...Don't talk to her, she's scary.' — Close enough for Yuna to hear. But her expression doesn't change. She's used to it.*

[classroom_seat_1]

  EN (Me): *It's next to Dain. Saved — or doomed — I'm not sure which, but at least it won't be quiet.*

[classroom_yuna_note_1]

  EN (Me): *A folded note lands on my desk. — I look back and the girl with the silver-white hair by the window is staring outside again. Despite her tattoo-covered arms — the handwriting on the note is neat.*

[classroom_yuna_note_5]

  EN (Me): *I glance toward Yuna. She's still looking out the window. But I think the tip of her ear just moved.*

[classroom_yuna_note_6]

  EN (Me): *Pretending not to care, or genuinely not caring. — Interesting.*


========== day1_2_lunch ==========

[lunch_start]
  KO ({name}): *점심시간. 종이 울렸다.*
  EN (Me): *Lunchtime. The bell rings.*

[lunch_start_2]
  KO ({name}): *교실을 나서니 복도가 학생들로 가득하다. 어디 가야 할지 모르겠다. 학식? 매점? 아니면 —*
  EN (Me): *I step out of the classroom and the hallway is packed with students. No idea where to go. Cafeteria? Snack bar? Or —*

[lunch_start_3]
  KO ({name}): *세 방향에서 동시에 신호가 온다.*
  EN (Me): *Signals come from three directions at once.*

[lunch_start_4]
  KO ({name}): *복도 끝에서 서연이 눈을 마주치고 손짓한다. 옥상 쪽을 가리키며.*
  EN (Me): *At the end of the hallway, Seoyeon catches my eye and gestures. Pointing toward the rooftop.*

[lunch_start_5]
  KO (다인): 야! 밥 먹었어?! 매점 가자! 내가 빵 사줄게... 진 사람이!
  EN (Dain): Hey! Have you eaten?! Let's hit the snack bar! I'll buy you bread... loser pays!

[lunch_start_6]
  KO ({name}): *그리고 유나가 가방만 들고 조용히 교실을 빠져나간다. 도서관 방향.*
  EN (Me): *And Yuna slips out of the classroom with just her bag. Heading toward the library.*

[lunch_start_7]
  KO ({name}): *세 갈래 길. 첫날부터 이런 선택을 해야 하다니.*
  EN (Me): *Three paths. Having to make a choice like this on day one.*

[lunch_start_7b]
  KO ({name}): *전 학교에서는 선택을 안 했다. 정확히는, 선택을 미루다가 타이밍을 놓쳤다. — 아, 또 이 생각. 입은 능글맞게 움직이는데, 정작 중요한 순간에는 굳어버리는 이 패턴. 나 자신이 제일 한심하다.*
  EN ({name}): *At my old school, I never chose. To be precise, I kept putting it off until the moment passed. — There it is again. My mouth moves smooth enough, but when it actually matters, it locks up. This pattern. I'm my own worst disappointment.*

[lunch_choice]
  KO ({name}): *누구를 따라갈까?*
  EN (Me): *Who do I follow?*
